# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 665–694

## Page 729 — IIIF Canvas #665

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0665.JP2` (2400 × 3095 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0665.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0665.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical treatment of subgaleal collections, post-healing pain at the cranial sutures, wounds of the forehead, nose, temples, cheek, and ears (including prosthetic noses and ears with glue recipe), and cheek-bone wounds.

## Page 730 — IIIF Canvas #666

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0666.JP2` (2400 × 2719 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0666.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0666.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Gargle waters for cheek/mouth wounds, remedies to heal facial slash wounds without scarring, salves to whiten red and disfigured facial scars, and a treatise on the dangers and prognosis of wounds of the neck and spinal marrow.

## Page 731 — IIIF Canvas #667

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0667.JP2` (2401 × 3075 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0667.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0667.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Conclusion of head wound care, followed by a section on wounds of the esophagus and windpipe ('Von den Wundten des Schlunds Vnd lufft röhr') with a cataplasm, gargle water, and nutrient clysters, plus a rubricated section on medicaments for previously mentioned wounds including pain relief and a head wound plaster.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | solte das blutt gestelt werden. nach abnemung | the blood should be stanched. After removal *— Note: Red-ink top horizontal and central vertical divider rules frame the columns* |
| 2 | mehr der bluttstellunge solle eine wundtsalben | further of the blood-stanching, a wound salve should |
| 3 | genohmen werden. weliche lieblich in die wund | be taken, which gently flows into the wound, |
| 4 | fließen lasse. und über die wunde lege das Kroll: | let it flow, and over the wound lay the Kroll: |
| 5 | sich Pflaster. oder das oppodeldoch. darüber | stich plaster, or the opodeldoc; over that |
| 6 | ein defensiue gelegt. wo große schmerzen muß | lay a defensive. Where there is great pain it must |
| 7 | es öffter überstrichen werden. ist aber khein | be smeared over more often. If however no |
| 8 | schmerzen mehr verhanden. kan es überlassen | pain is present anymore, it can be left off, |

| 10 | den auff gradt soll man mit gebierten wein öl | then along the spine one should anoint with distilled wine oil |

## Page 732 — IIIF Canvas #668

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0668.JP2` (2401 × 2747 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0668.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0668.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,500

Recipes for head wound plasters, sachets ('Matratzel oder säckel'), suture plasters, suture powder, green and red defensives, red and gray styptic powders ('Puluer Zum Plutt Stöln'), followed by the start of a new section on chest wounds ('Volgt Weider Von denen Wundten der Brust').

## Page 733 — IIIF Canvas #669

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0669.JP2` (2401 × 3133 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0669.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0669.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Continuation of chest wounds: symptoms, surgical drainage above the diaphragm, wound-washing decoction, internal wound drinks, bandaging techniques to prevent air entry, and prognosis of fatal chest wounds.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | erschrickt. oder wegen des luffts so durch die | frightens, or because of the air which is drawn in *— Note: Red-ink top horizontal and central vertical divider rules frame the columns* |
| 2 | wunden eingezogen wirdt. oder herauß geht. | through the wound or goes out; |
| 3 | alß wan ihme zu vil blutt auf das diaphragma | as when too much blood has settled on his diaphragm |
| 4 | gesessen hat. elbiges beschwerdt. es werde auch | and weighs it down. Such wounds |
| 5 | solliche wunden geschwindlich mit einen sucherle er= | are also quickly probed and found with a probe. |

Medical treatise page (page 134, recto) detailing the prognosis and surgical treatment of wounds to internal organs—specifically the heart, diaphragm, stomach, liver and spleen, gallbladder, and intestines—accompanied by six watercolor anatomical illustrations and rubricated headings.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Heart** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Red-tinted watercolor illustration of a human heart, positioned in the upper-left margin beside the section on wounds of the heart.

**Diaphragm** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Pinkish-red watercolor illustration of a dome-shaped diaphragm with two curved lower arches, positioned in the left margin beside the section on wounds of the diaphragm.

**Stomach** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Pinkish-tan watercolor illustration of a J-shaped human stomach with the esophageal opening at the top, positioned in the left margin beside the section on wounds of the stomach.

**Liver and Spleen** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Reddish-brown watercolor illustration of two bean-shaped organs (representing the liver and spleen) joined by a central vessel, positioned in the upper-left of the right column beside the section on wounds of the liver and spleen.

**Gallbladder** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Yellowish-green watercolor illustration of a pear-shaped gallbladder with its duct extending to the right, positioned in the middle of the right column beside the section on the injured gallbladder.

**Intestines** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Pinkish-brown watercolor illustration of coiled intestines, positioned in the lower-left of the right column beside the section on wounds of the intestines.

## Page 755 — IIIF Canvas #671

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0671.JP2` (2400 × 3111 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0671.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0671.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical and medical treatise on abdominal and urological wounds in 17th-century German. The left column continues instructions for suturing and treating wounds of the small and large intestines, including using a cannula/tube (Röhrl), applying a hemostatic powder (℞ mastic, frankincense, aloes, mumia, dragon's blood), and fomenting prolapsed, bloated intestines with warm wine. The right column concludes intestinal wound care (including references to iliac passion and sarsaparilla), then presents two rubricated sections accompanied by watercolor anatomical illustrations: 'Das Kress mesenterij' (wounds of the mesentery, with a crescent-shaped illustration) and 'Die Wunden der Niern vnnd Plasen' (wounds of the kidneys and bladder, with illustrations of two kidneys with renal vessels/ureters and a bladder), detailing diagnostic signs (bloody urine, urine leaking from the wound) and a kidney wound balm recipe.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Mesentery (Das Kress mesenterij)** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Tan/brownish-yellow crescent-shaped anatomical watercolor illustration in the left half of the right column, positioned next to the rubricated heading 'Das Kress mesenterij.' and its accompanying text.

**Kidneys and Bladder (Die Niern vnnd Plasen)** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Two anatomical watercolor illustrations in the left half of the right column, positioned next to the rubricated heading 'Die Wunden der Niern vnnd Plasen.': on the left, a pair of reddish-brown kidneys connected by red and blue blood vessels and descending ureters; on the right, a brownish-tan pouch-shaped urinary bladder with a curved neck.

## Page 138 — IIIF Canvas #672

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0672.JP2` (2401 × 2770 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0672.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0672.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical and medical treatise on wounds of various parts of the body (kidneys, bladder, hand/fist, great blood vessels and arteries, sinews, joints, muscle heads, poisoned weapons, transverse muscle wounds, jaw, skull, and chest/lungs), including a pill recipe for kidney and bladder wounds using burnt selenite (Marienglas) and Venetian turpentine.

## Page 739 — IIIF Canvas #673

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0673.JP2` (2400 × 3069 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0673.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0673.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical treatise on wound healing: healing wounds in elderly vs. plethoric/thin bodies, proper bandaging and protection from air, wounds of the external limbs (arms, legs, joints, hands, fingers) with instructions on tents/pledgets and joint mobility, and the beginning of a section on gunshot wounds.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0674.JP2` (2400 × 2804 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0674.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0674.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 742 — IIIF Canvas #676

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0676.JP2` (2400 × 2892 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0676.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0676.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation on wound corruption, pain, and inflammation remedies (oils, Crollius's Emplastrum Sticticum, henbane/nightshade ointment, laudanum opiatum), followed by sections on great inflammation and fever ('Von Grossen Enzüntung und fieber') and wound fever ('Von dem Wundt fieber') with its three types (wound fever, wound gall, restlessness) and diagnostic signs.

## Page 743 — IIIF Canvas #677

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0677.JP2` (2400 × 3124 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0677.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0677.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Causes of wound-sickness ('Die Ursachen diser Zue fäll khämen') including choleric complexion, anger, fear, negligence, and excessive bloodletting; followed by rules for prognosis ('Was aber bey disen Zuefall zu beobachten...') and treatment with diaphoretic antimony, bezoardicum, crabs' eyes, coral, hartshorn, and spirit of lignum sanctum.

## Page 744 — IIIF Canvas #678

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0678.JP2` (2400 × 2923 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0678.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0678.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation on inducing sweat and preventing liver inflammation with a hemp-seed/fenugreek cataplasm and wound dressings (St. John's oil, sugar of lead, Felix Würtz's brown ointment, Unguentum Althaeae), followed by a section on the second type of wound-sickness ('Die andere art der Wundtsucht Der Schaüder oder Wundtgall'), its blisters, prognosis, and treatment with diaphoretic antimony and Unguentum Mundificatum Compositum.

## Page 745 — IIIF Canvas #679

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0679.JP2` (2401 × 3132 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0679.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0679.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 746 — IIIF Canvas #680

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0680.JP2` (2400 × 2895 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0680.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0680.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Conclusion of wound gangrene and its cure in specific steps (using blessed thistle water, Unguentum Aegyptiacum, Würizi salve, and Opodeldoc plaster); section on itching/tickling of wounds (Von Hitzlen der Wunden) treated with laurel, radish, or turpentine oil; section on joint-water/synovia (Von dem Glid Wasser), distinguishing synovia from gluten, and its cure by wound balsams and plasters according to humor color.

## Page 747 — IIIF Canvas #681

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0681.JP2` (2400 × 3157 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0681.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0681.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation on joint-water colors (white, red, yellow) and their internal/external remedies, including Pilulae de succino Cratonis, morsels, fumigation, cataplasm of linseed/peas/cranium humanum, horse hoof with album Graecum, and roasted apple poultice; followed by a new section on stubborn/recalcitrant wounds (Von Widerspänigkeit der Wunden) caused by hidden internal humors, suppressed hemorrhoids, or latent syphilis, treated with a salve of crocus martis, salt, aloes, myrrh, and frankincense.

## Page 748 — IIIF Canvas #682

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0682.JP2` (2401 × 2921 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0682.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0682.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical treatise discussing wasting/atrophy (Schwindung) of wounded limbs, recipes for poultices and warm salves/ointments to restore wasted limbs, and the causes and treatment of paralysis (Lähmung) after the healing of a wounded limb.

## Page 751 — IIIF Canvas #685

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0685.JP2` (2401 × 3127 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0685.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0685.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 751 (recto) begins with a watercolor illustration of an open wooden apothecary medicine chest containing 15 compartments with stoppered vials, followed by a rubricated title on what a good surgeon should have and how his apothecary should be arranged. Recipes include Pulvis Contra Casum (powder against falls/bruising), Precipidierend Puluer (precipitating powder), Brust und Lungel Puluer (chest and lung powder), Purgier Puluer (purging powder), Pulvis Dysentericus (dysentery powder), Pulvis Contra Colicam (powder against colic), and Schwiz Puluer (sweating powder).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Was ain Guetter Chirurgo oder Wundarzt Haben soll Und Wie Sein apodekhen eingerichtet Sein Soll.** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor and ink illustration at the top of the left column depicting an open wooden apothecary chest with a hinged lid, keyhole on the front, and carrying handle on the left side. The interior is divided into three rows of five compartments (15 total), each holding a small stoppered glass or ceramic apothecary bottle.

## Page 755 — IIIF Canvas #689

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0689.JP2` (2401 × 3154 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0689.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0689.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 755 (recto) of MS.990 containing the conclusion of a gold/oil medicine, followed by a comprehensive inventory of plasters, ointments, oils, instruments, bandages, powders, and chemical/alchemical preparations required by a surgeon and field-barber (Was Weider ein Chirurigo & Feltzscherer Haben Soll), plus additional pieces (Andere Stukh) and anointing remedies (Zum Schmirzen).

## Page 756 — IIIF Canvas #690

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0690.JP2` (2400 × 3029 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0690.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0690.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 756 (verso) of MS.990 continuing the field-surgeon's inventory, followed by recipes for mouthwashes (Species Zum MundtWaser), clysters (Species Zum Gristiren), gangrene (Species Zum Kalten Brand), wound balsam (Wundt Bal.), fistula balsam (Fistel Balsam), burn and powder extinguishing (Brandt Und Puluer Löschung), styptic water (Aqua Stijptica), mercurial/gargle water (Mercurial. od gurgl Waser), and red throat/syphilitic water (Rotes Halb Waser).

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0691.JP2` (2400 × 3144 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0691.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0691.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recipes and lists of medicinal ointments (Unguenta), plasters (Emplastra), and poultices (Cataplasmata), including Basilicon Ointment, Anodyne Ointment, Diapalma Plaster, Arthritic Plaster, and a poultice against gangrene.

## Page 759 — IIIF Canvas #693

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0693.JP2` (2400 × 3161 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0693.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0693.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Surgical treatise on bone fractures (fractura, comminutio/morpha, fissura), their types, dangers, treatment, and diagnostic signs, illustrated at the top left by a watercolor of a bruised foot.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Bruised or fractured human foot** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor and ink illustration of a human left foot in lateral-dorsal view showing dark grey, brown, and reddish bruising/swelling around the ankle, heel, and instep.
